never executed always true always false

    1 {-# LANGUAGE RankNTypes, TypeFamilies, TypeOperators, FlexibleContexts, FlexibleInstances #-}
    2 module QuantLib.Internal.Type where
    3 import Foreign.Ptr(Ptr, FunPtr, nullPtr, nullFunPtr, freeHaskellFunPtr, plusPtr, castPtr)
    4 import Foreign.ForeignPtr(ForeignPtr, FinalizerPtr, newForeignPtr, withForeignPtr)
    5 import Foreign.C.Types(CUInt(..), CInt, CDouble(..))
    6 import Foreign.C.String(CString)
    7 import Foreign.Marshal.Array(withArray, peekArray, copyArray)
    8 import Foreign.Marshal.Utils(withMany, fillBytes)
    9 import Foreign.Storable(peek, sizeOf)

   11 import Control.Monad((>=>), when)
   12 import System.IO.Unsafe(unsafePerformIO)

   18 (<.>) :: Functor f => (b -> r) -> (a -> f b) -> a -> f r
   19 f1 <.> f2 = fmap f1 . f2
   20 
   21 -- STANDALONE TYPES
   22 newtype Standalone a = Standalone (ForeignPtr a)
   23 foreign import ccall "dynamic" callFinalizer :: FinalizerPtr a -> Ptr a -> IO ()
   24 class Finalizable a where
   25   finalize :: FinalizerPtr a
   26 peekStandalone :: Finalizable a => Ptr a -> IO (Standalone a)
   27 peekStandalone = Standalone <.> newForeignPtr finalize
   28 withStandalone :: Standalone a -> (Ptr a -> IO b) -> IO b
   29 withStandalone (Standalone p) = withForeignPtr p
   30 withMaybeStandalone :: Maybe (Standalone a) -> (Ptr a -> IO b) -> IO b
   31 withMaybeStandalone x f = maybe (f nullPtr) (`withStandalone` f) x
   32 withStandaloneArray :: (t -> Standalone a) -> [t] -> ((CUInt, Ptr (Ptr a)) -> IO b) -> IO b
   33 withStandaloneArray c x f = withMany withStandalone (map c x) (`withArray` (\px -> f (fromIntegral $ length x, px)))
   34 -- The name of a QuantLib object is fixed for its lifetime, so reading it through
   35 -- unsafePerformIO is safe; NOINLINE keeps GHC from duplicating or floating the C++
   36 -- call, matching how QuantLib.Settings guards its own unsafePerformIO sites.
   37 showStandalone :: (Ptr a -> IO CString) -> Standalone a -> String
   38 showStandalone f x = unsafePerformIO $ withStandalone x (f >=> peekDynString)
   39 {-# NOINLINE showStandalone #-}
   40 
   41 -- On `safe' vs `unsafe' imports, file-wide (this was an open TODO; it is settled):
   42 --   * The `&qlFreeX' finalizer imports below take a symbol *address*, not a call, so their
   43 --     `unsafe' annotation is inert. The call that matters is `callFinalizer' above, plus
   44 --     whatever the GC runs; both are safe.
   45 --   * Everything that runs QuantLib logic stays `safe'. Under the non-threaded RTS an
   46 --     `unsafe' call blocks GC and the scheduler for its whole duration, and pricing or
   47 --     bootstrapping is unbounded.
   48 --   * The qlXAsY upcast shims are the one legitimate `unsafe' candidate -- bare
   49 --     `ret(new QlY(*arg(o)))', no callback into Haskell, bounded work -- but they are
   50 --     already dominated by the QuantLib call they precede, so leave them `safe' absent a
   51 --     measurement; a per-shim rule would break the first time one grows logic.
   52 -- If a Haskell callback is ever passed into C++, every import on that path must be `safe'.
   53 --
   54 -- 'withCostFunction' below is the first such callback: it turns a Haskell @[Double] -> Double@
   55 -- into a C function pointer QuantLib's optimizer calls back into once per outer iteration (the
   56 -- whole parameter vector, not per component) -- the coarsened-callback shape documented in
   57 -- CLAUDE.md's "coarsen the language-boundary crossing" bullet, modeled on QuantLib-SWIG's own
   58 -- @PyCostFunction@ (@SWIG/functions.i@).
   59 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkCostFunPtr
   60   :: (Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> IO CDouble) -> IO (FunPtr (Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> IO CDouble))
   61 -- Build a C function pointer around a Haskell cost function for the duration of one 'optimize'
   62 -- call, freeing it with 'freeHaskellFunPtr' once the continuation returns, whether normally or
   63 -- via exception.
   64 withCostFunction :: (RealVector -> Double) -> (FunPtr (Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> IO CDouble) -> IO b) -> IO b
   65 withCostFunction f g = mask $ \restore -> do
   66   fp <- mkCostFunPtr call
   67   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
   68   where
   69     call xs n = do
   70       x <- borrowRealVector xs n
   71       pure (realToFrac (f x))
   72 
   73 -- 'fdmRollback' (QuantLib.Method) is the second callback-into-Haskell hook, driving
   74 -- @FdmBackwardSolver::rollback@ with a Haskell-defined 'FdmLinearOpComposite' (three callbacks:
   75 -- apply/apply_direction/solve_splitting) and an optional step condition -- see CLAUDE.md's
   76 -- "coarsen the language-boundary crossing" bullet and 'withCostFunction' above, whose
   77 -- mask\/finally\/freeHaskellFunPtr bracket this reuses verbatim, once per callback (four
   78 -- independent with-style marshallers, not a single tuple-returning one, so each composes as an
   79 -- ordinary c2hs @{#fun#}@ argument exactly like 'withCostFunction' does for 'optimize').
   80 --
   81 -- Every callback's raw C signature is @(in, n, <extra scalar args>, out)@: a caller-owned input
   82 -- buffer of length @n@, then whatever scalars the specific virtual takes (direction, the
   83 -- splitting parameter @s@, the @(t1,t2)@ time pair QuantLib's own @setTime@ stashes and threads
   84 -- through -- never a callback of its own, since it only ever stores two doubles), then a
   85 -- caller-owned *output* buffer also of length @n@. 'pokeBoundedFdmResult' is the one new safety
   86 -- property beyond 'withCostFunction' (which returns a single scalar, so has no analogous hazard):
   87 -- the C++ side's output buffer is exactly @n@ doubles, so a too-long Haskell result list must
   88 -- never be poked past that -- 'pokeBoundedFdmResult' truncates (a too-short list numerically
   89 -- wrong but safe, implicit-zero-padded).
   90 pokeBoundedFdmResult :: CUInt -> Ptr CDouble -> RealVector -> IO ()
   91 pokeBoundedFdmResult n out result = do
   92   let expected = fromIntegral n
   93       copied = min expected (V.length result)
   94   V.unsafeWith result $ \p -> copyArray out (castPtr p) copied
   95   when (copied < expected) $ fillBytes (out `plusPtr` (copied * sizeOf (undefined :: CDouble))) 0 ((expected - copied) * sizeOf (undefined :: CDouble))
   96 
   97 type FdmApplyFun = Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> CDouble -> CDouble -> Ptr CDouble -> IO ()
   98 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkFdmApplyFunPtr :: FdmApplyFun -> IO (FunPtr FdmApplyFun)
   99 -- |Wrap a Haskell @(t1,t2) -> grid -> grid'@ function (QuantLib's @FdmLinearOp::apply@\/
  100 -- @FdmLinearOpComposite::apply@, no direction argument) as a 'FdmApplyFun' C callback for the
  101 -- duration of one 'QuantLib.Method.fdmRollback' call.
  102 withFdmApply :: ((Double, Double) -> RealVector -> RealVector) -> (FunPtr FdmApplyFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  103 withFdmApply f g = mask $ \restore -> do
  104   fp <- mkFdmApplyFunPtr call
  105   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  106   where
  107     call xs n t1 t2 out = do
  108       x <- borrowRealVector xs n
  109       pokeBoundedFdmResult n out (f (realToFrac t1, realToFrac t2) x)
  110 
  111 type FdmApplyDirectionFun = Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> CUInt -> CDouble -> CDouble -> Ptr CDouble -> IO ()
  112 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkFdmApplyDirectionFunPtr :: FdmApplyDirectionFun -> IO (FunPtr FdmApplyDirectionFun)
  113 -- |Wrap a Haskell @direction -> (t1,t2) -> grid -> grid'@ function
  114 -- (@FdmLinearOpComposite::apply_direction@) as an 'FdmApplyDirectionFun' C callback.
  115 withFdmApplyDirection :: (Int -> (Double, Double) -> RealVector -> RealVector) -> (FunPtr FdmApplyDirectionFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  116 withFdmApplyDirection f g = mask $ \restore -> do
  117   fp <- mkFdmApplyDirectionFunPtr call
  118   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  119   where
  120     call xs n dir t1 t2 out = do
  121       x <- borrowRealVector xs n
  122       pokeBoundedFdmResult n out (f (fromIntegral dir) (realToFrac t1, realToFrac t2) x)
  123 
  124 type FdmSolveSplittingFun = Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> CUInt -> CDouble -> CDouble -> CDouble -> Ptr CDouble -> IO ()
  125 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkFdmSolveSplittingFunPtr :: FdmSolveSplittingFun -> IO (FunPtr FdmSolveSplittingFun)
  126 -- |Wrap a Haskell @direction -> s -> (t1,t2) -> grid -> grid'@ function
  127 -- (@FdmLinearOpComposite::solve_splitting@) as an 'FdmSolveSplittingFun' C callback.
  128 withFdmSolveSplitting :: (Int -> Double -> (Double, Double) -> RealVector -> RealVector) -> (FunPtr FdmSolveSplittingFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  129 withFdmSolveSplitting f g = mask $ \restore -> do
  130   fp <- mkFdmSolveSplittingFunPtr call
  131   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  132   where
  133     call xs n dir s t1 t2 out = do
  134       x <- borrowRealVector xs n
  135       pokeBoundedFdmResult n out (f (fromIntegral dir) (realToFrac s) (realToFrac t1, realToFrac t2) x)

  137 type FdmStepConditionFun = Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> CDouble -> Ptr CDouble -> IO ()
  138 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkFdmStepConditionFunPtr :: FdmStepConditionFun -> IO (FunPtr FdmStepConditionFun)
  139 -- |Wrap an optional Haskell @t -> grid -> grid'@ early-exercise\/barrier-style step condition
  140 -- (@StepCondition\<Array\>::applyTo@) as an 'FdmStepConditionFun' C callback, or a null 'FunPtr'
  141 -- when there is no step condition -- mirrors the existing @withMaybeX@ convention (e.g.
  142 -- 'withMaybeCurrency' above) of passing a null pointer for 'Nothing' rather than a separate
  143 -- present\/absent flag.
  144 withMaybeFdmStepCondition :: Maybe (Double -> RealVector -> RealVector) -> (FunPtr FdmStepConditionFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  145 withMaybeFdmStepCondition Nothing g = g nullFunPtr
  146 withMaybeFdmStepCondition (Just f) g = mask $ \restore -> do
  147   fp <- mkFdmStepConditionFunPtr call
  148   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  149   where
  150     call xs n t out = do
  151       x <- borrowRealVector xs n
  152       pokeBoundedFdmResult n out (f (realToFrac t) x)
  153 
  154 type FdmInnerValueFun = Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> CDouble -> IO CDouble
  155 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkFdmInnerValueFunPtr :: FdmInnerValueFun -> IO (FunPtr FdmInnerValueFun)
  156 -- |Wrap a Haskell @t -> nodeLocation -> value@ function as an 'FdmInnerValueFun' C callback, for
  157 -- either of @FdmInnerValueCalculator::innerValue@\/@avgInnerValue@ -- the one hook in this file
  158 -- that is a genuine, uncoarsened per-grid-node callback rather than a whole-grid one (see
  159 -- 'QuantLib.Method.fdmSolve' and its accompanying haddock for why no batched shape exists here,
  160 -- matching QuantLib-SWIG's own @FdmInnerValueCalculatorDelegate@). Reuses the same
  161 -- mask\/finally\/freeHaskellFunPtr bracket as 'withCostFunction' above; unlike the
  162 -- 'FdmApplyFun'-family callbacks, this one returns a single scalar so has no
  163 -- 'pokeBoundedFdmResult'-style output-length hazard.
  164 withFdmInnerValue :: (Double -> [Double] -> Double) -> (FunPtr FdmInnerValueFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  165 withFdmInnerValue f g = mask $ \restore -> do
  166   fp <- mkFdmInnerValueFunPtr call
  167   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  168   where
  169     call locPtr n t = do
  170       loc <- peekArray (fromIntegral n) locPtr
  171       pure (realToFrac (f (realToFrac t) (map realToFrac loc)))
  172 
  173 type FdmGridMappingFun = CDouble -> IO CDouble
  174 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkFdmGridMappingFunPtr :: FdmGridMappingFun -> IO (FunPtr FdmGridMappingFun)
  175 -- |Wrap a Haskell @Double -> Double@ function as an 'FdmGridMappingFun' C callback, for
  176 -- @FdmCellAveragingInnerValue@'s optional @gridMapping@ (@QuantLib.Method.withCustomCellAveragingInnerValue@)
  177 -- -- another genuine per-node callback (invoked from inside @avgInnerValueCalc@'s Simpson
  178 -- integration and from every @innerValue@ call), same reasoning as 'withFdmInnerValue' above.
  179 withFdmGridMapping :: (Double -> Double) -> (FunPtr FdmGridMappingFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  180 withFdmGridMapping f g = mask $ \restore -> do
  181   fp <- mkFdmGridMappingFunPtr call
  182   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  183   where
  184     call x = pure (realToFrac (f (realToFrac x)))
  185 
  186 type PayoffFun = CDouble -> IO CDouble
  187 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkPayoffFunPtr :: PayoffFun -> IO (FunPtr PayoffFun)
  188 -- |Wrap a Haskell @price -> value@ function as a 'PayoffFun' C callback, for
  189 -- @QuantLib.Internal.Common.withCustomPayoff@. Another genuine, uncoarsened callback: QuantLib's
  190 -- @Payoff::operator()@ takes one scalar price everywhere it is called -- per tree node in
  191 -- @DiscretizedVanillaOption@, inside @FdmCellAveragingInnerValue@'s per-cell Simpson integral, per
  192 -- path per exercise index in @AmericanPathPricer@ -- and nothing upstream batches an @Array@, so
  193 -- there is no whole-vector shape to coarsen to (same situation as 'withFdmInnerValue' above).
  194 withPayoffFun :: (Double -> Double) -> (FunPtr PayoffFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  195 withPayoffFun f g = mask $ \restore -> do
  196   fp <- mkPayoffFunPtr call
  197   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  198   where
  199     call x = pure (realToFrac (f (realToFrac x)))
  200 
  201 type BasketAccumulateFun = Ptr CDouble -> CUInt -> IO CDouble
  202 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkBasketAccumulateFunPtr :: BasketAccumulateFun -> IO (FunPtr BasketAccumulateFun)
  203 -- |Wrap a Haskell @underlyings -> accumulated@ function as a 'BasketAccumulateFun' C callback, for
  204 -- @QuantLib.Internal.Common.withCustomBasketPayoff@. Unlike 'withPayoffFun' this one /is/ already
  205 -- coarsened by upstream's own interface: @BasketPayoff::accumulate@ takes the whole underlying-state
  206 -- @Array@ per call, not one component at a time.
  207 withBasketAccumulateFun :: ([Double] -> Double) -> (FunPtr BasketAccumulateFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  208 withBasketAccumulateFun f g = mask $ \restore -> do
  209   fp <- mkBasketAccumulateFunPtr call
  210   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  211   where
  212     call xs n = do
  213       x <- peekArray (fromIntegral n) xs
  214       pure (realToFrac (f (map realToFrac x)))
  215 
  216 -- |The unary callback a Haskell-defined @DerivedQuote@ crosses on, for
  217 -- @QuantLib.Quote.withDerivedQuote@. Same C signature as 'PayoffFun', so it reuses
  218 -- 'withPayoffFun' rather than duplicating the @wrapper@ import; the alias exists so the quote
  219 -- bindings read in their own terms.
  220 type QuoteUnaryFun = PayoffFun
  221 
  222 -- |As 'QuoteUnaryFun', but for @MultiCompositeQuote@ (@QuantLib.Quote.withMultiCompositeQuote@):
  223 -- same C signature as 'BasketAccumulateFun', and coarsened the same way -- upstream hands the
  224 -- whole element vector over per evaluation.
  225 type QuoteArrayFun = BasketAccumulateFun
  226 
  227 type QuoteBinaryFun = CDouble -> CDouble -> IO CDouble
  228 foreign import ccall "wrapper" mkQuoteBinaryFunPtr :: QuoteBinaryFun -> IO (FunPtr QuoteBinaryFun)
  229 -- |Wrap a Haskell @value1 -> value2 -> value@ function as a 'QuoteBinaryFun' C callback, for
  230 -- @QuantLib.Quote.withCompositeQuote@. The genuinely new arity of the three: @CompositeQuote@'s
  231 -- @BinaryFunction@ takes both element values at once, so there is nothing to coarsen -- one
  232 -- crossing per @Quote::value()@ evaluation is already the whole computation.
  233 withQuoteBinaryFun :: (Double -> Double -> Double) -> (FunPtr QuoteBinaryFun -> IO b) -> IO b
  234 withQuoteBinaryFun f g = mask $ \restore -> do
  235   fp <- mkQuoteBinaryFunPtr call
  236   restore (g fp) `finally` freeHaskellFunPtr fp
  237   where
  238     call x y = pure (realToFrac (f (realToFrac x) (realToFrac y)))

  240 data CCalendar
  241 newtype Calendar = Calendar {getCCalendar :: Standalone CCalendar}
  242 instance Finalizable CCalendar where finalize = qlFreeCalendar
  243 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeCalendar" qlFreeCalendar :: FinalizerPtr CCalendar
  244 peekCalendar :: Ptr CCalendar -> IO Calendar
  245 peekCalendar = Calendar <.> peekStandalone
  246 withCalendar :: Calendar -> (Ptr CCalendar -> IO b) -> IO b
  247 withCalendar = withStandalone . getCCalendar
  248 foreign import ccall safe "ql.h qlCalendarName" qlCalendarName :: Ptr CCalendar -> IO CString
  249 instance Show Calendar where show x = showStandalone qlCalendarName (getCCalendar x)
  250 -- Equality by name, here and for the Currency/Region/DayCounter/Schedule instances
  251 -- below. This is deliberate: it is how QuantLib itself compares these types.
  252 instance Eq Calendar where x == y = show x == show y

  254 data CCurrency
  255 newtype Currency = Currency {getCCurrency :: Standalone CCurrency}
  256 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeCurrency" qlFreeCurrency :: FinalizerPtr CCurrency
  257 instance Finalizable CCurrency where finalize = qlFreeCurrency
  258 peekCurrency :: Ptr CCurrency -> IO Currency
  259 peekCurrency = Currency <.> peekStandalone
  260 withCurrency :: Currency -> (Ptr CCurrency -> IO b) -> IO b
  261 withCurrency = withStandalone . getCCurrency
  262 withMaybeCurrency :: Maybe Currency -> (Ptr CCurrency -> IO b) -> IO b
  263 withMaybeCurrency = withMaybeStandalone . (getCCurrency <$>)
  264 peekMaybeCurrency :: Ptr CCurrency -> IO (Maybe Currency)
  265 peekMaybeCurrency p
  266   | p == nullPtr = pure Nothing
  267   | otherwise = Just <$> peekCurrency p
  268 -- |Peek a 'Currency' out of a @Currency**@ out-parameter (as opposed to 'peekCurrency', which
  269 -- peeks it directly out of a @Currency*@ primary return).
  270 peekCurrencyPtr :: Ptr (Ptr CCurrency) -> IO Currency
  271 peekCurrencyPtr = peek >=> peekCurrency
  272 -- |Split a @(Double, Currency)@ cash amount (QuantLib's 'Money', per the @Period@-as-tuple
  273 -- convention) into the @(double, Currency*)@ pair of C arguments it marshals to, for use with
  274 -- the c2hs @&@ splitter -- the input-direction counterpart of 'peekCurrencyPtr'.
  275 withMoney :: (Double, Currency) -> ((CDouble, Ptr CCurrency) -> IO b) -> IO b
  276 withMoney (amount, ccy) f = withCurrency ccy (\p -> f (realToFrac amount, p))
  277 foreign import ccall safe "ql.h qlCurrencyName" qlCurrencyName :: Ptr CCurrency -> IO CString
  278 instance Show Currency where show x = showStandalone qlCurrencyName (getCCurrency x)
  279 instance Eq Currency where x == y = show x == show y

  381 peekDayCounter = DayCounter <.> peekStandalone

  388 data CSchedule
  389 newtype Schedule = Schedule {getCSchedule :: Standalone CSchedule}
  390 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeSchedule" qlFreeSchedule :: FinalizerPtr CSchedule
  391 instance Finalizable CSchedule where finalize = qlFreeSchedule
  392 peekSchedule :: Ptr CSchedule -> IO Schedule
  393 peekSchedule = Schedule <.> peekStandalone
  394 withSchedule :: Schedule -> (Ptr CSchedule -> IO b) -> IO b
  395 withSchedule = withStandalone . getCSchedule
  396 foreign import ccall safe "ql.h qlScheduleDates" qlScheduleDates :: Ptr CSchedule -> Ptr CUInt -> Ptr (Ptr CInt) -> IO ()
  397 showSchedule :: Schedule -> String
  398 showSchedule x = unsafePerformIO $ withSchedule x $ \p ->
  399   show <$> preArray (\(cp, ap) -> qlScheduleDates p cp ap >> peekDayArray cp ap)
  400 {-# NOINLINE showSchedule #-}
  401 
  402 instance Show Schedule where
  403   show = showSchedule
  404 instance Eq Schedule where
  405   x == y = show x == show y

  980 
  981 -- TYPE HIERARCHIES
  982 --
  983 -- Each hierarchy root below carries a haddock tree listing every member. Notation:
  984 --   indentation  parent/child
  985 --   `X*'         abstract *here*: hasquant binds no constructor returning an X, you only
  986 --                obtain one by upcasting. This is not the same as C++ abstractness and
  987 --                cannot be derived from it -- Option and Swap are concrete classes
  988 --                upstream but unconstructible here, while Quote/Index/TermStructure are
  989 --                pure-virtual upstream yet routinely returned by bindings. Marks are
  990 --                added where established; an unmarked node is not a claim of the opposite.
  991 --   `X + Y'      X also reaches secondary interface Y, via the standalone qlXAsY shim,
  992 --                materialized eagerly into a `Standalone Y' value by a per-leaf `xAsY'
  993 --                function (see CAffineModel' below), not via Upcastable.
  994 --   X (CFoo')    X's C type, given only where it is not the expected C<X>'.
  995 -- Payoff and Exercise are documented in the files that define them, not here.
  996 -- the original pointer to `a' with a way to marshal it to `b'
  997 -- The access/free pair IS derivable from the structure of `a' (each nested AnyOf layer is
  998 -- one upcast; the innermost ForeignPtr is identity or one upcast). It stays a stored
  999 -- dictionary because the alternative -- an `Access a b' class -- becomes a constraint at
 1000 -- every polymorphic use site, and c2hs emits an explicit signature for every {#fun#}: 363
 1001 -- of 880 hooks take a polymorphic `GenX a' and would each need a hand-written context,
 1002 -- which would also leak into public API signatures. It buys no correctness -- the smart
 1003 -- constructors below are already pinned by their result types.
 1004 data GenForeignPtr a b = GenForeignPtr {
 1005   ptr :: !a
 1006   , _access :: !(forall r. a -> (Ptr b -> IO r) -> IO r)
 1007   , _mayFree :: !(Maybe (Ptr b -> IO ())) -- `free' after upcast is needed
 1008 }
 1009 
 1010 freeUpcast :: Finalizable b => Ptr b -> IO ()
 1011 freeUpcast = callFinalizer finalize
 1012 
 1013 newtype AnyOf b a = AnyOf { getAnyOf :: GenForeignPtr a b }
 1014 newAnyOf :: (Upcastable b, Finalizable (Base b)) => GenForeignPtr a b -> GenForeignPtr (AnyOf b a) (Base b)
 1015 newAnyOf x = GenForeignPtr (AnyOf x)
 1016   (\(AnyOf i) f -> withGenForeignPtr i (upcast >=> f))
 1017   (Just freeUpcast)
 1018 
 1019 class Upcastable a where
 1020   type Base a
 1021   upcast :: Ptr a -> IO (Ptr (Base a))
 1022 
 1023 newGenForeignPtr :: (Finalizable a, Upcastable a, Finalizable (Base a)) => Ptr a -> IO (GenForeignPtr (ForeignPtr a) (Base a))
 1024 newGenForeignPtr x = do
 1025   fp <- newForeignPtr finalize x
 1026   pure $ GenForeignPtr fp (\a f -> withForeignPtr a (upcast >=> f)) (Just freeUpcast)
 1027 
 1028 newCastForeignPtr :: Finalizable a => Ptr a -> IO (GenForeignPtr (ForeignPtr a) a)
 1029 newCastForeignPtr x = do
 1030   fp <- newForeignPtr finalize x
 1031   pure $ GenForeignPtr fp withForeignPtr Nothing
 1032 
 1033 -- `access' performs the upcast, which allocates a fresh handle that `mfree' must release, so
 1034 -- acquiring it and installing the handler have to be atomic -- `mask' covers the upcast
 1035 -- happening inside `access', and `restore' hands `f' back the caller's masking state. This
 1036 -- is `bracket' semantics expressed around a continuation that
 1037 -- allocates internally. Nesting is fine: an inner level's `restore' only wraps the
 1038 -- continuation that contains the outer `restore', so `f' still runs unmasked.
 1039 withGenForeignPtr :: GenForeignPtr a b -> (Ptr b -> IO r) -> IO r
 1040 withGenForeignPtr (GenForeignPtr p access Nothing) f = access p f
 1041 withGenForeignPtr (GenForeignPtr p access (Just free)) f =
 1042   mask $ \restore -> access p $ \bp -> restore (f bp) `finally` free bp
 1043 
 1044 transferGenForeignPtr :: (Ptr b -> IO r) -> GenForeignPtr a b -> IO r
 1045 transferGenForeignPtr f (GenForeignPtr p access _) = access p f
 1046 
 1047 withGenArray :: (a -> (Ptr c -> IO r) -> IO r) -> [a] -> ((CUInt, Ptr (Ptr c)) -> IO r) -> IO r
 1048 withGenArray m x f = withMany m x (`withArray` (\p -> f (fromIntegral $ length x, p)))
 1049 
 1050 peel :: GenForeignPtr (AnyOf b a) c -> GenForeignPtr a b
 1051 peel = getAnyOf . ptr
 1052 
 1053 -- |Arrays of 'CommodityType'\/'UnitOfMeasure' values, first needed by 'QuantLib.Instrument.Energy'
 1054 -- for marshalling a 'QuantLib.Commodity.PricingPeriods' list to\/from its C-side
 1055 -- structure-of-parallel-arrays representation (@Quantity@'s @CommodityType@\/@UnitOfMeasure@
 1056 -- component, split out one array per field, alongside 'QuantLib.Internal.withDayArray'\/'peekDayArray'
 1057 -- for the date fields and 'QuantLib.Internal.withDoubleArray'\/'peekDoubleArray' for the amount) --
 1058 -- reusing 'withGenArray'\/'peekPtrArray' exactly as 'withBlackCalibrationHelperArray' does.
 1059 withCommodityTypeArray :: [CommodityType] -> ((CUInt, Ptr (Ptr CCommodityType)) -> IO b) -> IO b
 1060 withCommodityTypeArray = withGenArray withCommodityType
 1061 -- |Output side, first needed by 'QuantLib.Instrument.Energy.createPricingPeriods' (the only
 1062 -- producer of a fresh 'QuantLib.Commodity.PricingPeriods' list -- every constructor instead
 1063 -- *consumes* one via 'withCommodityTypeArray').
 1064 peekCommodityTypeArray :: Ptr CUInt -> Ptr (Ptr (Ptr CCommodityType)) -> IO [CommodityType]
 1065 peekCommodityTypeArray = peekPtrArray peekCommodityType
 1066 
 1067 withUnitOfMeasureArray :: [UnitOfMeasure] -> ((CUInt, Ptr (Ptr CUnitOfMeasure)) -> IO b) -> IO b
 1068 withUnitOfMeasureArray = withGenArray withUnitOfMeasure
 1069 peekUnitOfMeasureArray :: Ptr CUInt -> Ptr (Ptr (Ptr CUnitOfMeasure)) -> IO [UnitOfMeasure]
 1070 peekUnitOfMeasureArray = peekPtrArray peekUnitOfMeasure
 1071 
 1072 -- |A nullable-per-entry array of 'UnitOfMeasure's -- @SecondaryCosts@' per-entry unit of measure,
 1073 -- present only for its @CommodityUnitCost@ alternative (null for its @Money@ alternative).
 1074 withMaybeUnitOfMeasureArray :: [Maybe UnitOfMeasure] -> ((CUInt, Ptr (Ptr CUnitOfMeasure)) -> IO b) -> IO b
 1075 withMaybeUnitOfMeasureArray = withGenArray withMaybeUnitOfMeasure
 1076 
 1077 -- |An array of 'Currency' values -- @SecondaryCosts@'\/@SecondaryCostAmounts@'s per-entry currency.
 1078 withCurrencyArray :: [Currency] -> ((CUInt, Ptr (Ptr CCurrency)) -> IO b) -> IO b

 1080 peekCurrencyArray :: Ptr CUInt -> Ptr (Ptr (Ptr CCurrency)) -> IO [Currency]

 1115 -- let's at least provide a way to convert all quote classes to the most generic one

 1130 withRelinkableQuote :: RelinkableQuote -> (Ptr CRelinkableQuote' -> IO b) -> IO b
 1131 withRelinkableQuote = withGenQuote
 1132 withMaybeQuote :: Maybe (GenQuote q) -> (Ptr CQuote' -> IO b) -> IO b
 1133 withMaybeQuote x f = maybe (f nullPtr) (`withQuote` f) x
 1134 withQuoteArray :: [GenQuote q] -> ((CUInt, Ptr (Ptr CQuote')) -> IO b) -> IO b
 1135 withQuoteArray = withGenArray withQuote
 1136 withQuoteArrayRaw :: [GenQuote q] -> (Ptr (Ptr CQuote') -> IO b) -> IO b

 1296 -- | > CalibrationHelper
 1297 -- >   BlackCalibrationHelper*
 1298 -- >     SwaptionHelper
 1299 -- BlackCalibrationHelper is a proper one-AnyOf-layer family (mirrors GenSwap/GenOption under
 1300 -- GenInstrument), not a plain leaf directly under CalibrationHelper as before, so a concrete
 1301 -- subtype (SwaptionHelper) can be given its own getters without a runtime cast: SwaptionHelper's
 1302 -- own underlying()/swaption() need the real SwaptionHelper pointer, while the pre-existing
 1303 -- BlackCalibrationHelper-level accessors (times, blackPrice, impliedVolatility, ...) are
 1304 -- generalized to 'GenBlackCalibrationHelper bch' so they keep working on any leaf, SwaptionHelper
 1305 -- included, without an explicit upcast at each call site.
 1306 type CalibrationHelper = GenCalibrationHelper CCalibrationHelper
 1307 data CCalibrationHelper'
 1308 data CBlackCalibrationHelper'

 1310 type CCalibrationHelper = ForeignPtr CCalibrationHelper'

 1312 type CBlackCalibrationHelper = ForeignPtr CBlackCalibrationHelper'
 1313 type BlackCalibrationHelper = GenBlackCalibrationHelper CBlackCalibrationHelper
 1314 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeCalibrationHelper" qlFreeCalibrationHelper :: FinalizerPtr CCalibrationHelper'
 1315 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeBlackCalibrationHelper" qlFreeBlackCalibrationHelper :: FinalizerPtr CBlackCalibrationHelper'
 1316 instance Finalizable CCalibrationHelper' where finalize = qlFreeCalibrationHelper
 1317 instance Finalizable CBlackCalibrationHelper' where finalize = qlFreeBlackCalibrationHelper
 1318 foreign import ccall "ql.h qlBlackCalibrationHelperAsCalibrationHelper" qlBlackCalibrationHelperAsCalibrationHelper :: Ptr CBlackCalibrationHelper' -> IO (Ptr CCalibrationHelper')
 1319 instance Upcastable CBlackCalibrationHelper' where {type Base CBlackCalibrationHelper' = CCalibrationHelper'; upcast = qlBlackCalibrationHelperAsCalibrationHelper}
 1320 asCalibrationHelper :: GenCalibrationHelper ch -> IO CalibrationHelper
 1321 asCalibrationHelper = transferGenForeignPtr peekCalibrationHelper . getCalibrationHelper
 1322 peekCalibrationHelper :: Ptr CCalibrationHelper' -> IO CalibrationHelper
 1323 peekCalibrationHelper = GenCalibrationHelper <.> newCastForeignPtr
 1324 withCalibrationHelper :: GenCalibrationHelper ch -> (Ptr CCalibrationHelper' -> IO b) -> IO b
 1325 withCalibrationHelper = withGenForeignPtr . getCalibrationHelper
 1326 -- hands back the raw stored leaf pointer (e.g. Ptr CSwaptionHelper'); for a marshaller that
 1327 -- upcasts to the concrete Ptr CBlackCalibrationHelper', see 'withBlackCalibrationHelper' below.
 1328 withGenCalibrationHelper :: GenBlackCalibrationHelper (ForeignPtr ch) -> (Ptr ch -> IO b) -> IO b
 1329 withGenCalibrationHelper = withForeignPtr . ptr . peel . getCalibrationHelper
 1330 asBlackCalibrationHelper :: GenBlackCalibrationHelper bch -> IO BlackCalibrationHelper

 1339 withCalibrationHelperArray = withGenArray withCalibrationHelper

 1341 withBlackCalibrationHelperArray = withGenArray withBlackCalibrationHelper

 1616 -- | > TermStructure = GenTermStructure t
 1617 -- >  YieldTermStructure = GenYieldTermStructure y = GenTermStructure t
 1618 -- >    FittedBondDiscountCurve = GenYieldTermStructure ...
 1619 -- >    RelinkableYieldTermStructure = GenYieldTermStructure ...
 1620 -- (MultiCurve, below with the other standalone leaves, is not a YieldTermStructure member --
 1621 -- it manages a cycle of them, handing out 'YieldTermStructure' handles via addBootstrappedCurve
 1622 -- \/ addNonBootstrappedCurve. See its own definition's comment.)
 1623 -- >  VolatilityTermStructure
 1624 -- >    OptionletVolatilityStructure
 1625 -- >      RelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure
 1626 -- >    BlackVolTermStructure
 1627 -- >      BlackVarianceCurve
 1628 -- >      BlackVolatilitySurfaceDelta
 1629 -- >      RelinkableBlackVolTermStructure
 1630 -- >    SwaptionVolatilityStructure
 1631 -- >      RelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure
 1632 -- >      SabrSwaptionVolatilityCube
 1633 -- >      NoArbSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube
 1634 -- >      InterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCube
 1635 -- >    CapFloorTermVolatilityStructure*
 1636 -- >      CapFloorTermVolCurve
 1637 -- >      CapFloorTermVolSurface
 1638 -- >    BlackAtmVolCurve*
 1639 -- >      AbcdAtmVolCurve
 1640 -- >      BlackVolSurface*
 1641 -- >        SabrVolSurface
 1642 -- >    LocalVolTermStructure
 1643 -- >      GridModelLocalVolSurface
 1644 -- >    YoYOptionletVolatilitySurface
 1645 -- >    CPIVolatilitySurface
 1646 -- >  CallableBondVolatilityStructure
 1647 -- >  DefaultProbabilityTermStructure
 1648 -- >  ZeroInflationTermStructure
 1649 -- >  YoYInflationTermStructure
 1650 -- >  YoYCapFloorTermPriceSurface
 1651 -- >  CPICapFloorTermPriceSurface
 1652 -- >  CommodityCurve
 1653 type TermStructure = GenTermStructure CTermStructure
 1654 data CTermStructure'
 1655 data CVolatilityTermStructure'
 1656 data COptionletVolatilityStructure'
 1657 data CRelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure'
 1658 data CSwaptionVolatilityStructure'
 1659 data CRelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure'
 1660 data CSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube'
 1661 data CNoArbSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube'
 1662 data CInterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCube'
 1663 data CCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure'
 1664 data CCapFloorTermVolCurve'
 1665 data CCapFloorTermVolSurface'
 1666 data CLocalVolTermStructure'
 1667 data CGridModelLocalVolSurface'
 1668 data CAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl
 1669 data CYoYOptionletVolatilitySurface'
 1670 data CCPIVolatilitySurface'
 1671 data CBlackVolTermStructure'
 1672 data CRelinkableBlackVolTermStructure'
 1673 data CBlackVarianceCurve'
 1674 data CBlackVolatilitySurfaceDelta'
 1675 data CYieldTermStructure'
 1676 data CFittedBondDiscountCurve'
 1677 data CRelinkableYieldTermStructure'
 1678 data CCallableBondVolatilityStructure'
 1679 data CDefaultProbabilityTermStructure'
 1680 data CZeroInflationTermStructure'
 1681 data CYoYInflationTermStructure'
 1682 data CCommodityCurve'
 1683 newtype GenTermStructure t = GenTermStructure {getTermStructure :: GenForeignPtr t CTermStructure'}
 1684 type CTermStructure = ForeignPtr CTermStructure'
 1685 type GenYieldTermStructure y = GenTermStructure (AnyOf CYieldTermStructure' y)
 1686 type CYieldTermStructure = ForeignPtr CYieldTermStructure'
 1687 type YieldTermStructure = GenYieldTermStructure CYieldTermStructure
 1688 type CFittedBondDiscountCurve = ForeignPtr CFittedBondDiscountCurve'
 1689 type FittedBondDiscountCurve = GenYieldTermStructure CFittedBondDiscountCurve
 1690 type CRelinkableYieldTermStructure = ForeignPtr CRelinkableYieldTermStructure'
 1691 -- | A curve held behind a relinkable handle. It /is/ a 'YieldTermStructure' -- pass it
 1692 -- anywhere a curve is expected and it upcasts like any other hierarchy member, sharing its
 1693 -- @Link@ so that a later 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Yield.linkTo' reaches everything already
 1694 -- built on it.
 1695 type RelinkableYieldTermStructure = GenYieldTermStructure CRelinkableYieldTermStructure
 1696 type GenVolatilityTermStructure v = GenTermStructure (AnyOf CVolatilityTermStructure' v)
 1697 type CVolatilityTermStructure = ForeignPtr CVolatilityTermStructure'
 1698 type VolatilityTermStructure = GenVolatilityTermStructure CVolatilityTermStructure
 1699 type GenOptionletVolatilityStructure ov = GenVolatilityTermStructure (AnyOf COptionletVolatilityStructure' ov)
 1700 type COptionletVolatilityStructure = ForeignPtr COptionletVolatilityStructure'
 1701 type OptionletVolatilityStructure = GenOptionletVolatilityStructure COptionletVolatilityStructure
 1702 type CRelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure = ForeignPtr CRelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure'
 1703 -- | An optionlet vol surface held behind a relinkable handle. It /is/ an
 1704 -- 'OptionletVolatilityStructure' -- pass it anywhere one is expected and it upcasts like any
 1705 -- other hierarchy member, sharing its @Link@ so that a later
 1706 -- 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Volatility.linkOptionletVolTo' reaches everything already built on
 1707 -- it. Mirrors 'RelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure'.
 1708 type RelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure = GenOptionletVolatilityStructure CRelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure
 1709 type GenCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure c = GenVolatilityTermStructure (AnyOf CCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure' c)
 1710 type CCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure = ForeignPtr CCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure'
 1711 -- | The abstract root shared by 'ConstantCapFloorTermVolatility' (erased straight to this type at
 1712 -- construction, having no calc\/getter of its own beyond 'capFloorVolatilityForPeriod' et al.,
 1713 -- mirroring 'ConstantOptionletVolatility' \/\'OptionletVolatilityStructure'), and the two dedicated
 1714 -- leaves below. Promoted out of a flat 'VolatilityTermStructure' leaf (the way
 1715 -- 'YoYOptionletVolatilitySurface' still is) specifically so 'capFloorVolatilityForPeriod'\/
 1716 -- 'capFloorVolatilityForDate'\/'capFloorVolatilityForTime' -- declared on
 1717 -- @CapFloorTermVolatilityStructure@ upstream, not on 'VolatilityTermStructure' -- can be bound
 1718 -- generically without a @dynamic_pointer_cast@ in the shim.
 1719 type CapFloorTermVolatilityStructure = GenCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure CCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure
 1720 type CCapFloorTermVolCurve = ForeignPtr CCapFloorTermVolCurve'
 1721 -- | An ATM-only cap\/floor term vol curve (no strike dimension, unlike 'CapFloorTermVolSurface').
 1722 -- Gets its own dedicated leaf (rather than erasing to 'CapFloorTermVolatilityStructure' the way
 1723 -- 'ConstantCapFloorTermVolatility' does) so a future binding of @OptionletStripper2@ -- which takes
 1724 -- a concrete @Handle\<CapFloorTermVolCurve\>@ upstream -- has a type to reach for without another
 1725 -- breaking change here.
 1726 type CapFloorTermVolCurve = GenCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure CCapFloorTermVolCurve
 1727 type CCapFloorTermVolSurface = ForeignPtr CCapFloorTermVolSurface'
 1728 type CapFloorTermVolSurface = GenCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure CCapFloorTermVolSurface
 1729 type GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure sv = GenVolatilityTermStructure (AnyOf CSwaptionVolatilityStructure' sv)
 1730 type CSwaptionVolatilityStructure = ForeignPtr CSwaptionVolatilityStructure'
 1731 type SwaptionVolatilityStructure = GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure CSwaptionVolatilityStructure
 1732 type CRelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure = ForeignPtr CRelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure'
 1733 -- | A swaption vol surface held behind a relinkable handle. It /is/ a
 1734 -- 'SwaptionVolatilityStructure' -- pass it anywhere one is expected and it upcasts like any
 1735 -- other hierarchy member, sharing its @Link@ so that a later
 1736 -- 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Volatility.linkSwaptionVolTo' reaches everything already built on
 1737 -- it. Mirrors 'RelinkableBlackVolTermStructure'.
 1738 type RelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure = GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure CRelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure
 1739 type CSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube = ForeignPtr CSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube'
 1740 -- | A SABR-calibrated swaption vol cube. It /is/ a 'SwaptionVolatilityStructure' -- pass it
 1741 -- anywhere one is expected. Its own extra getters (sparse\/dense SABR parameters, market\/ATM-
 1742 -- calibrated vol cubes, ATM strike) are bound directly against this concrete type rather than
 1743 -- via a downcast: it has real calculations of its own beyond the generic interface, so per the
 1744 -- API-design rule in CLAUDE.md it earns a dedicated leaf instead of being collapsed into
 1745 -- 'SwaptionVolatilityStructure' the way 'swaptionVolatilityMatrix'' is.
 1746 type SabrSwaptionVolatilityCube = GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure CSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube
 1747 type CNoArbSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube = ForeignPtr CNoArbSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube'
 1748 -- | An arbitrage-free (Doust) SABR-calibrated swaption vol cube -- the same underlying
 1749 -- @XabrSwaptionVolatilityCube@ template as 'SabrSwaptionVolatilityCube', one model policy over
 1750 -- (@SwaptionVolCubeNoArbSabrModel@ instead of @SwaptionVolCubeSabrModel@), same dedicated-leaf
 1751 -- reasoning and identical getter surface.
 1752 type NoArbSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube = GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure CNoArbSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube
 1753 type CInterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCube = ForeignPtr CInterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCube'
 1754 -- | The non-SABR, linear-interpolation swaption vol cube. It /is/ a
 1755 -- 'SwaptionVolatilityStructure' -- pass it anywhere one is expected. Gets the same dedicated-leaf
 1756 -- treatment as 'SabrSwaptionVolatilityCube' for its 'atmStrike' getter (inherited, in upstream,
 1757 -- from the same abstract @SwaptionVolatilityCube@ base both concrete cubes share).
 1758 type InterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCube = GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure CInterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCube
 1759 -- | Black at-the-money (no-smile) volatility curve, abstract here (hasquant binds no
 1760 -- @qlBlackAtmVolCurve@ constructor -- @BlackAtmVolCurve@ has no bindable constructor upstream
 1761 -- either, only its concrete subclasses do). A sibling of 'OptionletVolatilityStructure'\/
 1762 -- 'CapFloorTermVolatilityStructure'\/'SwaptionVolatilityStructure' directly off
 1763 -- 'VolatilityTermStructure'. Reachable as a value via 'SabrVolSurface''s @atmCurve@ getter (any
 1764 -- concrete member may be held there), and as the argument type of 'sabrVolSurface'.
 1765 type GenBlackAtmVolCurve b = GenVolatilityTermStructure (AnyOf CBlackAtmVolCurve' b)
 1766 data CBlackAtmVolCurve'
 1767 type CBlackAtmVolCurve = ForeignPtr CBlackAtmVolCurve'
 1768 type BlackAtmVolCurve = GenBlackAtmVolCurve CBlackAtmVolCurve
 1769 -- | Black volatility (smile) surface: adds a strike\/smile dimension over 'BlackAtmVolCurve'.
 1770 -- Abstract here (no bindable constructor of its own -- only 'SabrVolSurface' constructs one in
 1771 -- this binding), but earns its own hierarchy level rather than folding into 'BlackAtmVolCurve'
 1772 -- (unlike @InterestRateVolSurface@, deliberately not given its own level -- see 'SabrVolSurface')
 1773 -- because its own calculation, @smileSection@, is the defining feature of the "surface" vs
 1774 -- "curve" distinction, not a thin pass-through inspector.
 1775 type GenBlackVolSurface b = GenBlackAtmVolCurve (AnyOf CBlackVolSurface' b)
 1776 data CBlackVolSurface'
 1777 type CBlackVolSurface = ForeignPtr CBlackVolSurface'
 1778 type BlackVolSurface = GenBlackVolSurface CBlackVolSurface
 1779 data CAbcdAtmVolCurve'
 1780 type CAbcdAtmVolCurve = ForeignPtr CAbcdAtmVolCurve'
 1781 -- | ABCD-parametric fit to a set of (tenor, quote) at-the-money vols. A dedicated
 1782 -- 'BlackAtmVolCurve' leaf (real calc\/getters of its own -- @a@\/@b@\/@c@\/@d@\/@rmsError@\/etc --
 1783 -- per the API-design rule in CLAUDE.md), one 'AnyOf' layer under 'GenBlackAtmVolCurve', same depth
 1784 -- as 'CapFloorTermVolCurve' under 'GenCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure'.
 1785 type AbcdAtmVolCurve = GenBlackAtmVolCurve CAbcdAtmVolCurve
 1786 data CSabrVolSurface'
 1787 type CSabrVolSurface = ForeignPtr CSabrVolSurface'
 1788 -- | SABR-smile surface built from an interest-rate index, an ATM 'BlackAtmVolCurve', and
 1789 -- per-tenor vol spreads. A dedicated 'BlackVolSurface' leaf (own getters: @atmCurve@,
 1790 -- @volatilitySpreads@; plus @index@\/@optionDateFromTenor@ folded in directly from upstream's
 1791 -- @InterestRateVolSurface@, which is not given its own hierarchy level here since
 1792 -- 'SabrVolSurface' is its only concrete member in this binding -- per CLAUDE.md's "don't mirror
 1793 -- the C++ hierarchy 1:1" rule). Two 'AnyOf' layers under 'GenBlackVolSurface' (mirrors
 1794 -- 'VanillaSwap' under 'FixedVsFloatingSwap' under 'GenSwap').
 1795 type SabrVolSurface = GenBlackVolSurface CSabrVolSurface
 1796 type GenLocalVolTermStructure lv = GenVolatilityTermStructure (AnyOf CLocalVolTermStructure' lv)

 1801 type AndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl = Standalone CAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl
 1802 type CYoYOptionletVolatilitySurface = ForeignPtr CYoYOptionletVolatilitySurface'
 1803 -- | A YoY-inflation optionlet vol surface, quoted via 'volatility'\/'totalVariance' at
 1804 -- (maturity, strike) pairs rather than QuantLib's usual (option date, tenor, strike) grid, since
 1805 -- inflation caplets observe a single index fixing rather than a forward rate. A plain
 1806 -- 'VolatilityTermStructure' leaf like 'CapFloorTermVolSurface', constructed and consumed via a
 1807 -- @Handle@ (mirroring 'OptionletVolatilityStructure', since it feeds
 1808 -- 'QuantLib.PricingEngine.yoyInflationBlackCapFloorEngine' et al. exactly the way
 1809 -- 'OptionletVolatilityStructure' feeds 'QuantLib.PricingEngine.blackCapFloorEngine'').
 1810 type YoYOptionletVolatilitySurface = GenVolatilityTermStructure CYoYOptionletVolatilitySurface
 1811 type CCPIVolatilitySurface = ForeignPtr CCPIVolatilitySurface'
 1812 -- | A CPI (zero-inflation) volatility surface, quoted via 'volatility'\/'totalVariance' at
 1813 -- (maturity, strike) pairs -- same shape as 'YoYOptionletVolatilitySurface', a plain
 1814 -- 'VolatilityTermStructure' leaf constructed and consumed via a @Handle@. Unlike
 1815 -- 'YoYOptionletVolatilitySurface' it feeds no pricing engine in QL 1.43: 'CPICapFloor' prices
 1816 -- purely off 'QuantLib.TermStructure.InflationVolatility.CPICapFloorTermPriceSurface' via
 1817 -- 'QuantLib.PricingEngine.interpolatingCPICapFloorEngine', and 'CPICouponPricer' (the type that
 1818 -- would consume this) is itself explicitly unfinished upstream for vol-dependent coupons (no
 1819 -- concrete descendant exists to bind, unlike 'YoYInflationCouponPricer's three) -- so this type
 1820 -- stands alone as a queryable surface, not (yet) as engine\/pricer plumbing.
 1821 type CPIVolatilitySurface = GenVolatilityTermStructure CCPIVolatilitySurface

 1890 -- A basket with a tranche-loss model, for CDO pricing.

 1902 withDigitalBasket = withBasket . digitalBasketAsBasket

 1930 type CYoYCapFloorTermPriceSurface = ForeignPtr CYoYCapFloorTermPriceSurface'
 1931 -- | Prices YoY cap\/floors by cap\/floor-surface intersection and put\/call parity, deriving an
 1932 -- ATM YoY swap curve as a side effect -- the market-data input the YoY optionlet stripper
 1933 -- ('QuantLib.TermStructure.InflationVolatility.kInterpolatedYoYOptionletVolatilitySurfaceBlack'
 1934 -- et al.) bootstraps from. A plain 'TermStructure' leaf, constructed and consumed by
 1935 -- @shared_ptr@ like 'CPICapFloorTermPriceSurface', never a @Handle@. Takes independent
 1936 -- 'Interpolation2D' (cap\/floor price grid) and 'Interpolation' (per-maturity) choices --

 1938 -- 'CPICapFloorTermPriceSurface's @InterpolatedCPICapFloorTermPriceSurface@, hence the separate
 1939 -- 2-D\/1-D pair rather than 'CPICapFloorTermPriceSurface's single 'Interpolation2D'.

 1941 data CCPICapFloorTermPriceSurface'
 1942 type CCPICapFloorTermPriceSurface = ForeignPtr CCPICapFloorTermPriceSurface'
 1943 -- | Prices CPI cap\/floors by interpolation and put\/call parity off a market strike\/maturity
 1944 -- price grid, not by any vol model (see 'CPICapFloor's own comment) -- a plain 'TermStructure'
 1945 -- leaf, constructed and consumed by @shared_ptr@ like 'CommodityCurve', never a @Handle@ (wrapped
 1946 -- into one at the point of use, e.g. 'QuantLib.PricingEngine.interpolatingCPICapFloorEngine').
 1947 -- Takes an 'Interpolation2D' choice for the cap\/floor price grid -- a different template
 1948 -- (@InterpolatedCPICapFloorTermPriceSurface@) from 'YoYCapFloorTermPriceSurface's
 1949 -- @InterpolatedYoYCapFloorTermPriceSurface@, hence its own single 2-D slot rather than
 1950 -- 'YoYCapFloorTermPriceSurface's separate 2-D\/1-D pair.

 2246 -- |Peek a possibly-null @CommodityCurve*@ -- @basisOfCurve_@ is a @nullptr@ 'shared_ptr' when no
 2247 -- basis curve has been set via 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Commodity.setCommodityCurveBasisOfCurve',
 2248 -- not an empty-'Data'-style placeholder (unlike 'peekMaybeCommodityType' et al.).
 2249 peekMaybeCommodityCurve :: Ptr CCommodityCurve' -> IO (Maybe CommodityCurve)
 2250 peekMaybeCommodityCurve p
 2251   | p == nullPtr = pure Nothing
 2252   | otherwise = Just <$> peekCommodityCurve p
 2253 
 2254 asYieldTermStructure :: GenYieldTermStructure y -> IO YieldTermStructure
 2255 asYieldTermStructure = transferGenForeignPtr peekYieldTermStructure . peel . getTermStructure
 2256 peekYieldTermStructure :: Ptr CYieldTermStructure' -> IO YieldTermStructure
 2257 peekYieldTermStructure = newCastForeignPtr >=> newGenYieldTermStructure
 2258 withYieldTermStructure :: GenYieldTermStructure y -> (Ptr CYieldTermStructure' -> IO b) -> IO b
 2259 withYieldTermStructure = withGenForeignPtr . peel . getTermStructure
 2260 withMaybeYieldTermStructure :: Maybe (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (Ptr CYieldTermStructure' -> IO b) -> IO b
 2261 withMaybeYieldTermStructure x f = maybe (f nullPtr) (`withYieldTermStructure` f) x
 2262 newGenYieldTermStructure :: GenForeignPtr y CYieldTermStructure' -> IO (GenYieldTermStructure y)
 2263 newGenYieldTermStructure = pure . GenTermStructure . newAnyOf

 2266 peekFittedBondDiscountCurve = newGenForeignPtr >=> newGenYieldTermStructure

 2268 peekRelinkableYieldTermStructure = newGenForeignPtr >=> newGenYieldTermStructure
 2269 -- | Reach the relinkable handle itself, for the operations that only it has ('linkTo',

 2271 -- which upcasts.

 3185 -- FixedVsFloatingSwap sits between Swap and VanillaSwap (upstream: VanillaSwap, OvernightIndexedSwap
 3186 -- and MultipleResetsSwap all derive from it, but only VanillaSwap is modelled through it here --
 3187 -- the others still upcast straight to Swap, collapsing the intermediate level as usual). It earns
 3188 -- its own family level (mirrors MultiAssetOption/MargrabeOption's one-more-AnyOf-layer shape)
 3189 -- because SwaptionHelper::underlying() (QuantLib/Model.chs) returns exactly this type, and its own
 3190 -- getters (fairRate, fairSpread, fixedLeg*, floatingLeg*) are inherited, not VanillaSwap-specific --
 3191 -- binding them generically over 'GenFixedVsFloatingSwap' avoids a cast to reach them from that
 3192 -- getter's result. FixedVsFloatingSwap itself is abstract upstream (pure virtual
 3193 -- setupFloatingArguments), so hasquant binds no constructor for it directly.
 3194 data CFixedVsFloatingSwap'
 3195 type GenFixedVsFloatingSwap f = GenSwap (AnyOf CFixedVsFloatingSwap' f)
 3196 type CFixedVsFloatingSwap = ForeignPtr CFixedVsFloatingSwap'
 3197 type FixedVsFloatingSwap = GenFixedVsFloatingSwap CFixedVsFloatingSwap
 3198 data CVanillaSwap'
 3199 type CVanillaSwap = ForeignPtr CVanillaSwap'
 3200 type VanillaSwap = GenFixedVsFloatingSwap CVanillaSwap
 3201 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeFixedVsFloatingSwap" qlFreeFixedVsFloatingSwap :: FinalizerPtr CFixedVsFloatingSwap'
 3202 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeVanillaSwap" qlFreeVanillaSwap :: FinalizerPtr CVanillaSwap'
 3203 instance Finalizable CFixedVsFloatingSwap' where finalize = qlFreeFixedVsFloatingSwap
 3204 instance Finalizable CVanillaSwap' where finalize = qlFreeVanillaSwap
 3205 foreign import ccall "ql.h qlFixedVsFloatingSwapAsSwap" qlFixedVsFloatingSwapAsSwap :: Ptr CFixedVsFloatingSwap' -> IO (Ptr CSwap')
 3206 foreign import ccall "ql.h qlVanillaSwapAsFixedVsFloatingSwap" qlVanillaSwapAsFixedVsFloatingSwap :: Ptr CVanillaSwap' -> IO (Ptr CFixedVsFloatingSwap')
 3207 instance Upcastable CFixedVsFloatingSwap' where {type Base CFixedVsFloatingSwap' = CSwap'; upcast = qlFixedVsFloatingSwapAsSwap}
 3208 instance Upcastable CVanillaSwap' where {type Base CVanillaSwap' = CFixedVsFloatingSwap'; upcast = qlVanillaSwapAsFixedVsFloatingSwap}
 3209 asFixedVsFloatingSwap :: GenFixedVsFloatingSwap f -> IO FixedVsFloatingSwap
 3210 asFixedVsFloatingSwap = transferGenForeignPtr peekFixedVsFloatingSwap . peel . peel . getInstrument
 3211 peekFixedVsFloatingSwap :: Ptr CFixedVsFloatingSwap' -> IO FixedVsFloatingSwap
 3212 peekFixedVsFloatingSwap = newCastForeignPtr >=> newGenFixedVsFloatingSwap
 3213 withFixedVsFloatingSwap :: GenFixedVsFloatingSwap f -> (Ptr CFixedVsFloatingSwap' -> IO b) -> IO b
 3214 withFixedVsFloatingSwap = withGenForeignPtr . peel . peel . getInstrument
 3215 newGenFixedVsFloatingSwap :: GenForeignPtr f CFixedVsFloatingSwap' -> IO (GenFixedVsFloatingSwap f)
 3216 newGenFixedVsFloatingSwap = pure . GenInstrument . newAnyOf . newAnyOf
 3217 peekVanillaSwap :: Ptr CVanillaSwap' -> IO VanillaSwap
 3218 peekVanillaSwap = newGenForeignPtr >=> newGenFixedVsFloatingSwap
 3219 withVanillaSwap :: VanillaSwap -> (Ptr CVanillaSwap' -> IO b) -> IO b
 3220 withVanillaSwap = withForeignPtr . ptr . peel . peel . getInstrument
 3221 
 3222 -- ConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwap sits between Swap and its two owned leaves
 3223 -- (ConstNotionalCrossCurrencyBasisSwap, ConstNotionalCrossCurrencyFixedVsFloatingSwap). It earns
 3224 -- its own family level (mirrors FixedVsFloatingSwap/VanillaSwap just above) because its own
 3225 -- getters (legCurrency, inCcyLegBPS, inCcyLegNPV, npvDateDiscounts) are inherited by both leaves
 3226 -- and are bound generically over 'GenConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwap', while each leaf keeps its
 3227 -- own engine-dispatched getters (fairPaySpread/fairRecSpread, fairRate/fairSpread) reachable only
 3228 -- through the real leaf pointer. Unlike FixedVsFloatingSwap, the base class is concrete upstream
 3229 -- and hasquant binds its own 2-leg/N-leg constructors directly at this level.

 3605 -- Commodity/EnergyCommodity are abstract-here: Commodity's own constructor is never called
 3606 -- directly upstream (every concrete instrument goes through EnergyCommodity), and
 3607 -- EnergyCommodity::quantity() is pure virtual, so neither binds a constructor here -- both are
 3608 -- reachable only as upcast targets once a Stage-6 leaf (EnergyFuture, EnergyVanillaSwap,
 3609 -- EnergyBasisSwap) exists. Commodity::secondaryCosts()/EnergyCommodity::commodityType() are plain,
 3610 -- never-mutated echoes of each class's own constructor argument (commodity.hpp/energycommodity.hpp's
 3611 -- inline getters each just `return foo_;`) -- not bound, per CLAUDE.md's trivial-getter rule.
 3612 -- secondaryCostAmounts()/pricingErrors()/addPricingError are genuine (mutable, computed during
 3613 -- pricing); their generalized 'withCommodity' accessor and marshalling live below, next to the
 3614 -- Stage-6 leaves that finally give them a producer to verify against.
 3615 data CCommodity'
 3616 type GenCommodity c = GenInstrument (AnyOf CCommodity' c)
 3617 type CCommodity = ForeignPtr CCommodity'
 3618 type Commodity = GenCommodity CCommodity
 3619 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeCommodity" qlFreeCommodity :: FinalizerPtr CCommodity'
 3620 instance Finalizable CCommodity' where finalize = qlFreeCommodity
 3621 foreign import ccall "ql.h qlCommodityAsInstrument" qlCommodityAsInstrument :: Ptr CCommodity' -> IO (Ptr CInstrument')
 3622 instance Upcastable CCommodity' where {type Base CCommodity' = CInstrument'; upcast = qlCommodityAsInstrument}
 3623 
 3624 data CEnergyCommodity'
 3625 type GenEnergyCommodity e = GenCommodity (AnyOf CEnergyCommodity' e)
 3626 type CEnergyCommodity = ForeignPtr CEnergyCommodity'
 3627 type EnergyCommodity = GenEnergyCommodity CEnergyCommodity
 3628 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeEnergyCommodity" qlFreeEnergyCommodity :: FinalizerPtr CEnergyCommodity'
 3629 instance Finalizable CEnergyCommodity' where finalize = qlFreeEnergyCommodity
 3630 foreign import ccall "ql.h qlEnergyCommodityAsCommodity" qlEnergyCommodityAsCommodity :: Ptr CEnergyCommodity' -> IO (Ptr CCommodity')
 3631 instance Upcastable CEnergyCommodity' where {type Base CEnergyCommodity' = CCommodity'; upcast = qlEnergyCommodityAsCommodity}
 3632 
 3633 -- |Generalizes 'Commodity's base-level getters (secondaryCostAmounts, pricingErrors,
 3634 -- addPricingError) across every leaf in the Commodity\/EnergyCommodity\/EnergySwap subtree -- the
 3635 -- same move as 'withFixedVsFloatingSwap' generalizing its own base-level getters. One peel:
 3636 -- Commodity is the (only, so far) 'AnyOf' layer directly under 'GenInstrument' here.
 3637 withCommodity :: GenCommodity c -> (Ptr CCommodity' -> IO b) -> IO b
 3638 withCommodity = withGenForeignPtr . peel . getInstrument
 3639 
 3640 -- |'newGenEnergyCommodity' wraps the 2 'AnyOf' layers (Commodity, EnergyCommodity) shared by every
 3641 -- 'EnergyCommodity' leaf -- the same helper role 'newGenFixedVsFloatingSwap' plays for 'VanillaSwap'.
 3642 newGenEnergyCommodity :: GenForeignPtr e CEnergyCommodity' -> IO (GenEnergyCommodity e)
 3643 newGenEnergyCommodity = pure . GenInstrument . newAnyOf . newAnyOf
 3644 
 3645 -- |Generalizes 'EnergyCommodity's one pure-virtual interface method, @quantity()@, across every
 3646 -- leaf (EnergyFuture, EnergyVanillaSwap, EnergyBasisSwap): one shim, dispatched virtually on the
 3647 -- C++ side, rather than a per-leaf binding -- EnergyFuture's own override is a plain echo of its
 3648 -- constructor argument, but EnergySwap's is a real computed sum over its pricing periods
 3649 -- (energyswap.cpp), so the binding as a whole is not a redundant echo even though one leaf's
 3650 -- override happens to be.
 3651 withEnergyCommodity :: GenEnergyCommodity e -> (Ptr CEnergyCommodity' -> IO b) -> IO b
 3652 withEnergyCommodity = withGenForeignPtr . peel . peel . getInstrument
 3653 
 3654 -- |'EnergyFuture': a leaf directly under 'EnergyCommodity' (Stage 6). 2 peels reach
 3655 -- 'CEnergyFuture'' -- through the Commodity and EnergyCommodity layers -- the same depth
 3656 -- 'VanillaSwap' needs under 'GenFixedVsFloatingSwap' (Swap + FixedVsFloatingSwap).
 3657 data CEnergyFuture'
 3658 type CEnergyFuture = ForeignPtr CEnergyFuture'
 3659 type EnergyFuture = GenEnergyCommodity CEnergyFuture
 3660 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeEnergyFuture" qlFreeEnergyFuture :: FinalizerPtr CEnergyFuture'
 3661 instance Finalizable CEnergyFuture' where finalize = qlFreeEnergyFuture
 3662 foreign import ccall "ql.h qlEnergyFutureAsEnergyCommodity" qlEnergyFutureAsEnergyCommodity :: Ptr CEnergyFuture' -> IO (Ptr CEnergyCommodity')
 3663 instance Upcastable CEnergyFuture' where {type Base CEnergyFuture' = CEnergyCommodity'; upcast = qlEnergyFutureAsEnergyCommodity}
 3664 peekEnergyFuture :: Ptr CEnergyFuture' -> IO EnergyFuture
 3665 peekEnergyFuture = newGenForeignPtr >=> newGenEnergyCommodity
 3666 withEnergyFuture :: EnergyFuture -> (Ptr CEnergyFuture' -> IO b) -> IO b
 3667 withEnergyFuture = withForeignPtr . ptr . peel . peel . getInstrument
 3668 
 3669 -- |'EnergySwap' is abstract-here: it binds a public constructor upstream, but never overrides
 3670 -- 'performCalculations', so calling it directly falls through to 'Instrument's null-engine
 3671 -- @QL_REQUIRE@ and throws -- reachable only as an upcast target from 'EnergyVanillaSwap'\/
 3672 -- 'EnergyBasisSwap', exactly like 'FixedVsFloatingSwap'\/'VanillaSwap'. Its own accessors
 3673 -- (calendar, payCurrency, receiveCurrency, pricingPeriods, dailyPositions, paymentCashFlows,
 3674 -- commodityType, quantity) are bound generically over 'GenEnergySwap' in
 3675 -- 'QuantLib.Instrument.Energy' so both leaves inherit them for free, mirroring
 3676 -- 'GenFixedVsFloatingSwap's fairRate\/fixedLeg*\/floatingLeg* getters.

 3679 type CEnergySwap = ForeignPtr CEnergySwap'
 3680 type EnergySwap = GenEnergySwap CEnergySwap

 3684 instance Upcastable CEnergySwap' where {type Base CEnergySwap' = CEnergyCommodity'; upcast = qlEnergySwapAsEnergyCommodity}
 3685 newGenEnergySwap :: GenForeignPtr s CEnergySwap' -> IO (GenEnergySwap s)
 3686 newGenEnergySwap = pure . GenInstrument . newAnyOf . newAnyOf . newAnyOf
 3687 withEnergySwap :: GenEnergySwap s -> (Ptr CEnergySwap' -> IO b) -> IO b
 3688 withEnergySwap = withGenForeignPtr . peel . peel . peel . getInstrument

 3714 -- |'CommodityCashFlow': a standalone 'CashFlow' subclass, following the same
 3715 -- ZeroInflationCashFlow\/CPICashFlow\/EquityCashFlow precedent (no polymorphic @CashFlow@ family is
 3716 -- modelled here -- see those types in 'QuantLib.CashFlow' -- each concrete cash flow class is its
 3717 -- own standalone foreign-pointer type instead).
 3718 data CCommodityCashFlow
 3719 newtype CommodityCashFlow = CommodityCashFlow {getCCommodityCashFlow :: Standalone CCommodityCashFlow}
 3720 foreign import ccall unsafe "ql.h &qlFreeCommodityCashFlow" qlFreeCommodityCashFlow :: FinalizerPtr CCommodityCashFlow
 3721 instance Finalizable CCommodityCashFlow where finalize = qlFreeCommodityCashFlow
 3722 peekCommodityCashFlow :: Ptr CCommodityCashFlow -> IO CommodityCashFlow
 3723 peekCommodityCashFlow = CommodityCashFlow <.> peekStandalone
 3724 withCommodityCashFlow :: CommodityCashFlow -> (Ptr CCommodityCashFlow -> IO b) -> IO b
 3725 withCommodityCashFlow = withStandalone . getCCommodityCashFlow
 3726 -- |An array of freshly-constructed 'CommodityCashFlow's -- 'EnergySwap.paymentCashFlows()''s
 3727 -- @map<Date, shared_ptr<CommodityCashFlow>>@, read as a plain list (each element's own
 3728 -- 'QuantLib.Instrument.Energy.commodityCashFlowDate' already carries the map key, so it isn't
 3729 -- duplicated as a separate tuple field).
 3730 peekCommodityCashFlowArray :: Ptr CUInt -> Ptr (Ptr (Ptr CCommodityCashFlow)) -> IO [CommodityCashFlow]
 3731 peekCommodityCashFlowArray = peekPtrArray peekCommodityCashFlow
